0020: 
0021:     def process(
0022:         self,
0023:         document: Document,
0024:     ) -> PipelineResult:
0025: 
0026:         loaded = PDFLoader().load(document)
0027:         parsed = PDFParser().parse(loaded)
0028: 
0029:         chunked = PDFChunker().chunk(parsed)
0030:         metadata = PDFMetadata().extract(chunked)
0031:         indexed = PDFIndexer().index(metadata)
0032: 
0033:         requests = EmbeddingBuilder().build(indexed)
0034: 
0035:         indexed.metadata["embedding_requests"] = requests
0036: 
0037:         return PipelineResult(
0038:             source_document=document,
0039:             indexed_document=indexed,
0040:             success=True,
0041:         )

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0028: class Document(ElementProxy):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0029:     """WordprocessingML (WML) document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0030: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0031:     Not intended to be constructed directly. Use :func:`docx.Document` to open or create
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0032:     a document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0033:     """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0034: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0035:     def __init__(self, element: CT_Document, part: DocumentPart):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0036:         super(Document, self).__init__(element)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0037:         self._element = element
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0038:         self._part = part
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0039:         self.__body = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0040: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0041:     def add_comment(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0042:         self,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0043:         runs: Run | Sequence[Run],
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0044:         text: str | None = "",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0045:         author: str = "",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0046:         initials: str | None = "",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0047:     ) -> Comment:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0048:         """Add a comment to the document, anchored to the specified runs.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0049: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0050:         `runs` can be a single `Run` object or a non-empty sequence of `Run` objects. Only the
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0051:         first and last run of a sequence are used, it's just more convenient to pass a whole
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0052:         sequence when that's what you have handy, like `paragraph.runs` for example. When `runs`
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0053:         contains a single `Run` object, that run serves as both the first and last run.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0054: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0055:         A comment can be anchored only on an even run boundary, meaning the text the comment
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0056:         "references" must be a non-zero integer number of consecutive runs. The runs need not be
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0057:         _contiguous_ per se, like the first can be in one paragraph and the last in the next
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0058:         paragraph, but all runs between the first and the last will be included in the reference.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0059: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0060:         The comment reference range is delimited by placing a `w:commentRangeStart` element before
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0061:         the first run and a `w:commentRangeEnd` element after the last run. This is why only the
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0062:         first and last run are required and why a single run can serve as both first and last.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0063:         Word works out which text to highlight in the UI based on these range markers.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0064: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0065:         `text` allows the contents of a simple comment to be provided in the call, providing for
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0066:         the common case where a comment is a single phrase or sentence without special formatting
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0067:         such as bold or italics. More complex comments can be added using the returned `Comment`
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0068:         object in much the same way as a `Document` or (table) `Cell` object, using methods like
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0069:         `.add_paragraph()`, .add_run()`, etc.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0070: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0071:         The `author` and `initials` parameters allow that metadata to be set for the comment.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0072:         `author` is a required attribute on a comment and is the empty string by default.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0073:         `initials` is optional on a comment and may be omitted by passing |None|, but Word adds an
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0074:         `initials` attribute by default and we follow that convention by using the empty string
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0075:         when no `initials` argument is provided.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0076:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0077:         # -- normalize `runs` to a sequence of runs --
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0078:         runs = [runs] if isinstance(runs, Run) else runs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0079:         first_run = runs[0]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0080:         last_run = runs[-1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0081: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0082:         # -- Note that comments can only appear in the document part --
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0083:         comment = self.comments.add_comment(text=text, author=author, initials=initials)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0084: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0085:         # -- let the first run orchestrate placement of the comment range start and end --
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0086:         first_run.mark_comment_range(last_run, comment.comment_id)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0087: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0088:         return comment
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0089: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0090:     def add_heading(self, text: str = "", level: int = 1):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0091:         """Return a heading paragraph newly added to the end of the document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0092: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0093:         The heading paragraph will contain `text` and have its paragraph style
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0094:         determined by `level`. If `level` is 0, the style is set to `Title`. If `level`
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0095:         is 1 (or omitted), `Heading 1` is used. Otherwise the style is set to `Heading
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0096:         {level}`. Raises |ValueError| if `level` is outside the range 0-9.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0097:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0098:         if not 0 <= level <= 9:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0099:             raise ValueError("level must be in range 0-9, got %d" % level)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0100:         style = "Title" if level == 0 else "Heading %d" % level
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0101:         return self.add_paragraph(text, style)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0102: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0103:     def add_page_break(self):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0104:         """Return newly |Paragraph| object containing only a page break."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0105:         paragraph = self.add_paragraph()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0106:         paragraph.add_run().add_break(WD_BREAK.PAGE)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0107:         return paragraph

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0109:     def add_paragraph(self, text: str = "", style: str | ParagraphStyle | None = None) -> Paragraph:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0110:         """Return paragraph newly added to the end of the document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0111: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0112:         The paragraph is populated with `text` and having paragraph style `style`.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0113: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0114:         `text` can contain tab (``\\t``) characters, which are converted to the
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0115:         appropriate XML form for a tab. `text` can also include newline (``\\n``) or
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0116:         carriage return (``\\r``) characters, each of which is converted to a line
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0117:         break.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0118:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0119:         return self._body.add_paragraph(text, style)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0121:     def add_picture(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0122:         self,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0123:         image_path_or_stream: str | IO[bytes],
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0124:         width: int | Length | None = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0125:         height: int | Length | None = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0126:     ):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0127:         """Return new picture shape added in its own paragraph at end of the document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0128: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0129:         The picture contains the image at `image_path_or_stream`, scaled based on
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0130:         `width` and `height`. If neither width nor height is specified, the picture
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0131:         appears at its native size. If only one is specified, it is used to compute a
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0132:         scaling factor that is then applied to the unspecified dimension, preserving the
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0133:         aspect ratio of the image. The native size of the picture is calculated using
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0134:         the dots-per-inch (dpi) value specified in the image file, defaulting to 72 dpi
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0135:         if no value is specified, as is often the case.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0136:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0137:         run = self.add_paragraph().add_run()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0138:         return run.add_picture(image_path_or_stream, width, height)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0331:     def __str__(self) -> str:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:0333:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2827:     def __contains__(self, loc) -> bool:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2828:         if type(loc) is int:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2829:             if loc < self.page_count:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2830:                 return True
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2831:             return False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2832:         if type(loc) not in (tuple, list) or len(loc) != 2:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2833:             return False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2834:         chapter, pno = loc
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2835:         if (0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2836:                 or not isinstance(chapter, int)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2837:                 or chapter < 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2838:                 or chapter >= self.chapter_count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2839:                 ):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2840:             return False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2841:         if (0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2842:                 or not isinstance(pno, int)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2843:                 or pno < 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2844:                 or pno >= self.chapter_page_count(chapter)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2845:                 ):

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2849:     def __delitem__(self, i)->None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2850:         if not self.is_pdf:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2851:             raise ValueError("is no PDF")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2852:         if type(i) is int:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2853:             return self.delete_page(i)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2854:         if type(i) in (list, tuple, range):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2855:             return self.delete_pages(i)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2856:         if type(i) is not slice:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2857:             raise ValueError("bad argument type")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2858:         pc = self.page_count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2859:         start = i.start if i.start else 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2860:         stop = i.stop if i.stop else pc
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2861:         step = i.step if i.step else 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2862:         while start < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2863:             start += pc
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2864:         if start >= pc:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2865:             raise ValueError("bad page number(s)")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2866:         while stop < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2867:             stop += pc
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2868:         if stop > pc:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2869:             raise ValueError("bad page number(s)")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:2870:         return self.delete_pages(range(start, stop, step))

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3092:     def _delToC(self):

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3108:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3137:     def _do_links(

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3144:         """Insert links contained in copied page range into destination PDF.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3145:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3147:         have been previously executed.

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3152:         # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3153:         def cre_annot(lnk, xref_dst, pno_src, ctm):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3154:             """Create annotation object string for a passed-in link."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3155: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3156:             r = lnk["from"] * ctm  # rect in PDF coordinates
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3157:             rect = _format_g(tuple(r))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3158:             if lnk["kind"] == LINK_GOTO:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3159:                 txt = annot_skel["goto1"]  # annot_goto
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3160:                 idx = pno_src.index(lnk["page"])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3161:                 p = lnk["to"] * ctm  # target point in PDF coordinates
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3162:                 annot = txt(xref_dst[idx], p.x, p.y, lnk["zoom"], rect)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3163: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3164:             elif lnk["kind"] == LINK_GOTOR:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3165:                 if lnk["page"] >= 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3166:                     txt = annot_skel["gotor1"]  # annot_gotor
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3167:                     pnt = lnk.get("to", Point(0, 0))  # destination point
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3168:                     if type(pnt) is not Point:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3169:                         pnt = Point(0, 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3170:                     annot = txt(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3171:                         lnk["page"],
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3172:                         pnt.x,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3173:                         pnt.y,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3174:                         lnk["zoom"],
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3175:                         lnk["file"],
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3176:                         lnk["file"],
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3177:                         rect,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3178:                     )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3179:                 else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3180:                     txt = annot_skel["gotor2"]  # annot_gotor_n
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3181:                     to = get_pdf_str(lnk["to"])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3182:                     to = to[1:-1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3183:                     f = lnk["file"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3184:                     annot = txt(to, f, rect)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3185: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3186:             elif lnk["kind"] == LINK_LAUNCH:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3187:                 txt = annot_skel["launch"]  # annot_launch
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3188:                 annot = txt(lnk["file"], lnk["file"], rect)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3189: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3190:             elif lnk["kind"] == LINK_URI:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3191:                 txt = annot_skel["uri"]  # annot_uri
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3192:                 annot = txt(lnk["uri"], rect)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3193: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3194:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3195:                 annot = ""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3196: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3197:             return annot
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3198: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3199:         # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3200: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3201:         # validate & normalize parameters
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3202:         if from_page < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3203:             fp = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3204:         elif from_page >= doc2.page_count:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3205:             fp = doc2.page_count - 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3206:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3207:             fp = from_page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3208:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3212:             tp = to_page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3213:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3215:             raise ValueError("'start_at' must be >= 0")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3216:         sa = start_at
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3217: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3218:         incr = 1 if fp <= tp else -1  # page range could be reversed
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3219: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3220:         # lists of source / destination page numbers
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3221:         pno_src = list(range(fp, tp + incr, incr))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3222:         pno_dst = [sa + i for i in range(len(pno_src))]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3223:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3225:         xref_src = []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3226:         xref_dst = []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3227:         for i in range(len(pno_src)):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3228:             p_src = pno_src[i]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3229:             p_dst = pno_dst[i]

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3231:             new_xref = doc1.page_xref(p_dst)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3232:             xref_src.append(old_xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3233:             xref_dst.append(new_xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3234: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3235:         # create the links for each copied page in destination PDF
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3236:         for i in range(len(xref_src)):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3237:             page_src = doc2[pno_src[i]]  # load source page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3238:             links = page_src.get_links()  # get all its links
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3239:             #log(f'{pno_src=}')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3240:             #log(f'{type(page_src)=}')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3241:             #log(f'{page_src=}')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3242:             #log(f'{=i len(links)}')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3243:             if len(links) == 0:  # no links there
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3244:                 page_src = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3245:                 continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3246:             page_dst = doc1[pno_dst[i]]  # load destination page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3247:             
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3248:             # In our call above to page_src.get_links(), we end up in
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3249:             # fz_load_links(). This extracts the raw rects (encoded as strings
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3250:             # such as `/Rect[10 782 40 822]`) and multiplies them by page_ctm
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3251:             # from pdf_page_transform().
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3252:             #
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3253:             # We want to recreate the original raw rects, so we need to
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3254:             # multiply by inverse of page_ctm. This fixes #4958.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3255:             ctm = mupdf.FzMatrix()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3256:             page_src_pdf_document = _as_pdf_page(page_src)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3257:             mupdf.pdf_page_transform(page_src_pdf_document, mupdf.FzRect(0), ctm)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3258:             ictm = Matrix(mupdf.fz_invert_matrix(ctm))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3259: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3260:             link_tab = []  # store all link definitions here
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3261:             for l in links:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3262:                 if l["kind"] == LINK_GOTO and (l["page"] not in pno_src):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3263:                     continue  # GOTO link target not in copied pages
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3264:                 annot_text = cre_annot(l, xref_dst, pno_src, ictm)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3265:                 if annot_text:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3266:                     link_tab.append(annot_text)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3267:             if link_tab != []:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3297:         def join_widgets(pdf, acro_fields, xref1, xref2, name):

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3310:             def re_target(pdf, acro_fields, xref1, kids1, xref2, kids2):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3311:                 """Merge widget in xref2 into "Kids" list of widget xref1.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3312:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3328:                     widget.pdf_dict_del(PDF_NAME("T"))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3329:                     widget.pdf_dict_put(PDF_NAME("Parent"), w1_ind)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3330:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3332:                     kids1.pdf_array_push(w2_ind)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3333:                 else:  # copy source kids to target kids
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3334:                     for i in range(kids2.pdf_array_len()):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3335:                         kid = kids2.pdf_array_get(i)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3336:                         kid.pdf_dict_put(PDF_NAME("Parent"), w1_ind)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3337:                         kid_ind = mupdf.pdf_new_indirect(pdf, kid.pdf_to_num(), 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3338:                         kids1.pdf_array_push(kid_ind)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3340:             def new_target(pdf, acro_fields, xref1, w1, xref2, w2, name):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3341:                 """Make new "Parent" for two widgets with same name.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3342: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3343:                 Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3344:                     xref1, w1: first widget
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3345:                     xref2, w2: second widget
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3346:                     name: field name
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3347:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3403:         def get_kids(parent, kids_list):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3404:             """Return xref list of leaf kids for a parent.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3405: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3406:             Call with an empty list.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3407:             """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3408:             kids = mupdf.pdf_dict_get(parent, PDF_NAME("Kids"))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3409:             if not kids.pdf_is_array():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3410:                 return kids_list
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3411:             for i in range(kids.pdf_array_len()):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3412:                 kid = kids.pdf_array_get(i)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3413:                 if mupdf.pdf_is_dict(mupdf.pdf_dict_get(kid, PDF_NAME("Kids"))):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3414:                     kids_list = get_kids(kid, kids_list)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3415:                 else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3416:                     kids_list.append(kid.pdf_to_num())
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3417:             return kids_list

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3419:         def kids_xrefs(widget):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3420:             """Get the xref of top "Parent" and the list of leaf widgets."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3421:             kids_list = []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3422:             parent = mupdf.pdf_dict_get(widget, PDF_NAME("Parent"))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3423:             parent_xref = parent.pdf_to_num()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3424:             if parent_xref == 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3425:                 return parent_xref, kids_list
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3426:             kids_list = get_kids(parent, kids_list)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3427:             return parent_xref, kids_list

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3429:         def deduplicate_names(pdf, acro_fields, join_duplicates=False):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3430:             """Handle any widget name duplicates caused by the merge."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3431:             names = {}  # key is a widget name, value a list of widgets having it.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3432: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3433:             # extract all names and widgets in "AcroForm/Fields"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3434:             for i in range(mupdf.pdf_array_len(acro_fields)):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3435:                 wobject = mupdf.pdf_array_get(acro_fields, i)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3436:                 xref = wobject.pdf_to_num()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3437: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3438:                 # extract widget name and collect widget(s) using it
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3439:                 T = mupdf.pdf_dict_get_text_string(wobject, PDF_NAME("T"))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3440:                 xrefs = names.get(T, [])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3441:                 xrefs.append(xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3442:                 names[T] = xrefs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3443: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3444:             for name, xrefs in names.items():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3445:                 if len(xrefs) < 2:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3446:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3447:                 xref0, xref1 = xrefs[:2]  # only exactly 2 should occur!
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3448:                 if join_duplicates:  # combine fields with equal names
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3449:                     join_widgets(pdf, acro_fields, xref0, xref1, name)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3450:                 else:  # make field names unique
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3451:                     newname = name + f" [{xref1}]"  # append this to the name
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3452:                     wobject = mupdf.pdf_load_object(pdf, xref1)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3732:     def _embfile_upd(self, idx, buffer_=None, filename=None, ufilename=None, desc=None):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3733:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3734:         xref = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3735:         names = mupdf.pdf_dict_getl(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3736:                 mupdf.pdf_trailer(pdf),

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3745:         if not filespec.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3746:             RAISEPY( "bad PDF: no /EF object", JM_Exc_FileDataError)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3747:         res = JM_BufferFromBytes(buffer_)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3748:         if buffer_ and buffer_.m_internal and not res.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3749:             raise TypeError( MSG_BAD_BUFFER)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3750:         if res.m_internal and buffer_ and buffer_.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3751:             JM_update_stream(pdf, filespec, res, 1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3752:             # adjust /DL and /Size parameters
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3753:             len, _ = mupdf.fz_buffer_storage(res)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3754:             l = mupdf.pdf_new_int(len)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3755:             mupdf.pdf_dict_put(filespec, PDF_NAME('DL'), l)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3756:             mupdf.pdf_dict_putl(filespec, l, PDF_NAME('Params'), PDF_NAME('Size'))

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3758:         if filename:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3759:             mupdf.pdf_dict_put_text_string(entry, PDF_NAME('F'), filename)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3760: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3761:         if ufilename:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3762:             mupdf.pdf_dict_put_text_string(entry, PDF_NAME('UF'), ufilename)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3763: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3764:         if desc:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3765:             mupdf.pdf_dict_put_text_string(entry, PDF_NAME('Desc'), desc)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3766:         return xref

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3768:     def _extend_toc_items(self, items):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3769:         """Add color info to all items of an extended TOC list."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3770:         if self.is_closed:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3771:             raise ValueError("document closed")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3772:         if g_use_extra:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3773:             return extra.Document_extend_toc_items( self.this, items)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3774:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3775:         zoom = "zoom"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3776:         bold = "bold"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3777:         italic = "italic"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3778:         collapse = "collapse"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3779: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3780:         root = mupdf.pdf_dict_get(mupdf.pdf_trailer(pdf), PDF_NAME('Root'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3781:         if not root.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3782:             return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3783:         olroot = mupdf.pdf_dict_get(root, PDF_NAME('Outlines'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3784:         if not olroot.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3785:             return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3786:         first = mupdf.pdf_dict_get(olroot, PDF_NAME('First'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3787:         if not first.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3788:             return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3789:         xrefs = []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3790:         xrefs = JM_outline_xrefs(first, xrefs)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3791:         n = len(xrefs)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3792:         m = len(items)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3793:         if not n:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3794:             return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3795:         if n != m:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3796:             raise IndexError( "internal error finding outline xrefs")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3797: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3798:         # update all TOC item dictionaries
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3799:         for i in range(n):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3800:             xref = int(xrefs[i])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3801:             item = items[i]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3802:             itemdict = item[3]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3803:             if not isinstance(itemdict, dict):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3804:                 raise ValueError( "need non-simple TOC format")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3805:             itemdict[dictkey_xref] = xrefs[i]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3806:             bm = mupdf.pdf_load_object(pdf, xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3807:             flags = mupdf.pdf_to_int( mupdf.pdf_dict_get(bm, PDF_NAME('F')))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3808:             if flags == 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3809:                 itemdict[italic] = True
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3810:             elif flags == 2:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3811:                 itemdict[bold] = True
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3812:             elif flags == 3:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3813:                 itemdict[italic] = True
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3814:                 itemdict[bold] = True
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3815:             count = mupdf.pdf_to_int( mupdf.pdf_dict_get(bm, PDF_NAME('Count')))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3816:             if count < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:3817:                 itemdict[collapse] = True

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4310:     def convert_to_pdf(self, from_page=0, to_page=-1, rotate=0):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4311:         """Convert document to a PDF, selecting page range and optional rotation. Output bytes object."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4312:         if self.is_closed or self.is_encrypted:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4313:             raise ValueError("document closed or encrypted")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4314:         fz_doc = self.this
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4315:         fp = from_page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4316:         tp = to_page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4317:         srcCount = mupdf.fz_count_pages(fz_doc)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4318:         if fp < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4319:             fp = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4320:         if fp > srcCount - 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4321:             fp = srcCount - 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4322:         if tp < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4323:             tp = srcCount - 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4324:         if tp > srcCount - 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4325:             tp = srcCount - 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4326:         len0 = len(JM_mupdf_warnings_store)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4327:         doc = JM_convert_to_pdf(fz_doc, fp, tp, rotate)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4328:         len1 = len(JM_mupdf_warnings_store)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4329:         for i in range(len0, len1):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4330:             message(f'{JM_mupdf_warnings_store[i]}')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4331:         return doc

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4372:     def delete_pages(self, *args, **kw):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4373:         """Delete pages from a PDF.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4374: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4375:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4376:             Either keywords 'from_page'/'to_page', or two integers to
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4377:             specify the first/last page to delete.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4378:             Or a list/tuple/range object, which can contain arbitrary
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4379:             page numbers.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4380:             Or a single integer page number.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4381:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4382:         if not self.is_pdf:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4383:             raise ValueError("is no PDF")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4384:         if self.is_closed:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4385:             raise ValueError("document closed")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4386: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4387:         page_count = self.page_count  # page count of document
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4388:         f = t = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4389:         if kw:  # check if keywords were used
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4390:             if args:  # then no positional args are allowed
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4391:                 raise ValueError("cannot mix keyword and positional argument")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4392:             f = kw.get("from_page", -1)  # first page to delete
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4393:             t = kw.get("to_page", -1)  # last page to delete
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4394:             while f < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4395:                 f += page_count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4396:             while t < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4397:                 t += page_count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4398:             if not f <= t < page_count:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4399:                 raise ValueError("bad page number(s)")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4400:             numbers = tuple(range(f, t + 1))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4401:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4402:             if len(args) > 2 or args == []:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4403:                 raise ValueError("need 1 or 2 positional arguments")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4404:             if len(args) == 2:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4405:                 f, t = args
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4406:                 if not (type(f) is int and type(t) is int):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4407:                     raise ValueError("both arguments must be int")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4408:                 if f > t:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4409:                     f, t = t, f
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4410:                 if not f <= t < page_count:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4411:                     raise ValueError("bad page number(s)")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4412:                 numbers = tuple(range(f, t + 1))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4413:             elif isinstance(args[0], int):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4414:                 pno = args[0]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4415:                 while pno < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4416:                     pno += page_count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4417:                 numbers = (pno,)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4418:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4419:                 numbers = tuple(args[0])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4420: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4421:         numbers = list(map(int, set(numbers)))  # ensure unique integers
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4422:         if numbers == []:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4423:             message("nothing to delete")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4424:             return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4425:         numbers.sort()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4426:         if numbers[0] < 0 or numbers[-1] >= page_count:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4427:             raise ValueError("bad page number(s)")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4428:         frozen_numbers = frozenset(numbers)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4429:         toc = self.get_toc()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4430:         for i, xref in enumerate(self.get_outline_xrefs()):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4431:             if toc[i][2] - 1 in frozen_numbers:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4432:                 self._remove_toc_item(xref)  # remove target in PDF object
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4433: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4434:         self._remove_links_to(frozen_numbers)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4435: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4436:         for i in reversed(numbers):  # delete pages, last to first
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4437:             self._delete_page(i)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4438: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4439:         self._reset_page_refs()

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4568:     def extract_font(self, xref=0, info_only=0, named=None):

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4703:     def fullcopy_page(self, pno, to=-1):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4704:         """Make a full page duplicate."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4705:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4706:         page_count = mupdf.pdf_count_pages( pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4707:         try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4708:             if (not _INRANGE(pno, 0, page_count - 1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4709:                     or not _INRANGE(to, -1, page_count - 1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4710:                     ):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4711:                 raise ValueError( MSG_BAD_PAGENO)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4714: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4715:             page2 = mupdf.pdf_deep_copy_obj( page1)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4717: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4718:             # copy annotations, but remove Popup and IRT types
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4719:             if old_annots.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4720:                 n = mupdf.pdf_array_len( old_annots)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4721:                 new_annots = mupdf.pdf_new_array( pdf, n)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4722:                 for i in range(n):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4723:                     o = mupdf.pdf_array_get( old_annots, i)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4724:                     subtype = mupdf.pdf_dict_get( o, PDF_NAME('Subtype'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4725:                     if mupdf.pdf_name_eq( subtype, PDF_NAME('Popup')):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4726:                         continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4727:                     if mupdf.pdf_dict_gets( o, "IRT").m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4728:                         continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4729:                     copy_o = mupdf.pdf_deep_copy_obj( mupdf.pdf_resolve_indirect( o))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4730:                     xref = mupdf.pdf_create_object( pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4731:                     mupdf.pdf_update_object( pdf, xref, copy_o)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4732:                     copy_o = mupdf.pdf_new_indirect( pdf, xref, 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4733:                     mupdf.pdf_dict_del( copy_o, PDF_NAME('Popup'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4734:                     mupdf.pdf_dict_del( copy_o, PDF_NAME('P'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4735:                     mupdf.pdf_array_push( new_annots, copy_o)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4736:                 mupdf.pdf_dict_put( page2, PDF_NAME('Annots'), new_annots)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4737: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4738:             # copy the old contents stream(s)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4739:             res = JM_read_contents( page1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4740: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4741:             # create new /Contents object for page2
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4742:             if res and res.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4743:                 #contents = mupdf.pdf_add_stream( pdf, mupdf.fz_new_buffer_from_copied_data( b"  ", 1), NULL, 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4744:                 contents = mupdf.pdf_add_stream( pdf, mupdf.fz_new_buffer_from_copied_data( b" "), mupdf.PdfObj(), 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4745:                 JM_update_stream( pdf, contents, res, 1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4746:                 mupdf.pdf_dict_put( page2, PDF_NAME('Contents'), contents)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4747: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4748:             # now insert target page, making sure it is an indirect object
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4749:             xref = mupdf.pdf_create_object( pdf)   # get new xref
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4750:             mupdf.pdf_update_object( pdf, xref, page2) # store new page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4751: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4752:             page2 = mupdf.pdf_new_indirect( pdf, xref, 0)  # reread object
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4753:             mupdf.pdf_insert_page( pdf, to, page2) # and store the page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4754:         finally:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4759:     def get_char_widths(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4760:             doc: 'Document',
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4761:             xref: int,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4762:             limit: int = 256,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4763:             idx: int = 0,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4764:             fontdict: OptDict = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4765:             ) -> list:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4766:         """Get list of glyph information of a font.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4767: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4768:         Notes:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4769:             Must be provided by its XREF number. If we already dealt with the
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4770:             font, it will be recorded in doc.FontInfos. Otherwise we insert an
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4771:             entry there.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4772:             Finally we return the glyphs for the font. This is a list of
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4773:             (glyph, width) where glyph is an integer controlling the char
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4774:             appearance, and width is a float controlling the char's spacing:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4775:             width * fontsize is the actual space.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4776:             For 'simple' fonts, glyph == ord(char) will usually be true.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4777:             Exceptions are 'Symbol' and 'ZapfDingbats'. We are providing data for these directly here.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4778:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4779:         fontinfo = CheckFontInfo(doc, xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4780:         if fontinfo is None:  # not recorded yet: create it
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4781:             if fontdict is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4782:                 name, ext, stype, asc, dsc = utils._get_font_properties(doc, xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4783:                 fontdict = {
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4784:                     "name": name,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4785:                     "type": stype,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4786:                     "ext": ext,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4787:                     "ascender": asc,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4788:                     "descender": dsc,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4789:                 }
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4790:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4791:                 name = fontdict["name"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4792:                 ext = fontdict["ext"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4793:                 stype = fontdict["type"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4794:                 ordering = fontdict["ordering"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4795:                 simple = fontdict["simple"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4796: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4797:             if ext == "":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4798:                 raise ValueError("xref is not a font")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4799: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4800:             # check for 'simple' fonts
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4801:             if stype in ("Type1", "MMType1", "TrueType"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4802:                 simple = True
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4803:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4804:                 simple = False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4805: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4806:             # check for CJK fonts
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4807:             if name in ("Fangti", "Ming"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4808:                 ordering = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4809:             elif name in ("Heiti", "Song"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4810:                 ordering = 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4811:             elif name in ("Gothic", "Mincho"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4812:                 ordering = 2
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4813:             elif name in ("Dotum", "Batang"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4814:                 ordering = 3
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4815:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4816:                 ordering = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4817: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4818:             fontdict["simple"] = simple
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4819: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4820:             if name == "ZapfDingbats":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4821:                 glyphs = zapf_glyphs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4822:             elif name == "Symbol":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4823:                 glyphs = symbol_glyphs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4824:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4825:                 glyphs = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4826: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4827:             fontdict["glyphs"] = glyphs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4828:             fontdict["ordering"] = ordering
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4829:             fontinfo = [xref, fontdict]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4830:             doc.FontInfos.append(fontinfo)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4831:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4832:             fontdict = fontinfo[1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4833:             glyphs = fontdict["glyphs"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4834:             simple = fontdict["simple"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4835:             ordering = fontdict["ordering"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4836: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4837:         if glyphs is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4838:             oldlimit = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4839:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4840:             oldlimit = len(glyphs)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4841: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4842:         mylimit = max(256, limit)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4843: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4844:         if mylimit <= oldlimit:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4845:             return glyphs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4846: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4847:         if ordering < 0:  # not a CJK font
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4848:             glyphs = doc._get_char_widths(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4849:                 xref, fontdict["name"], fontdict["ext"], fontdict["ordering"], mylimit, idx
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4850:             )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4851:         else:  # CJK fonts use char codes and width = 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4852:             glyphs = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4853: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4854:         fontdict["glyphs"] = glyphs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4855:         fontinfo[1] = fontdict
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4856:         UpdateFontInfo(doc, fontinfo)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4857: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4858:         return glyphs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4859:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4917:     def get_oc(doc: 'Document', xref: int) -> int:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4981:     def get_ocmd(doc: 'Document', xref: int) -> dict:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4982:         """Return the definition of an OCMD (optional content membership dictionary).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4983: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4984:         Recognizes PDF dict keys /OCGs (PDF array of OCGs), /P (policy string) and
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4985:         /VE (visibility expression, PDF array). Via string manipulation, this
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4986:         info is converted to a Python dictionary with keys "xref", "ocgs", "policy"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4987:         and "ve" - ready to recycle as input for 'set_ocmd()'.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4988:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4989: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4990:         if xref not in range(doc.xref_length()):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4991:             raise ValueError("bad xref")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4992:         text = doc.xref_object(xref, compressed=True)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4993:         if "/Type/OCMD" not in text:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4994:             raise ValueError("bad object type")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4995:         textlen = len(text)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4996: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4997:         p0 = text.find("/OCGs[")  # look for /OCGs key
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4998:         p1 = text.find("]", p0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:4999:         if p0 < 0 or p1 < 0:  # no OCGs found
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5000:             ocgs = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5001:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5002:             ocgs = text[p0 + 6 : p1].replace("0 R", " ").split()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5003:             ocgs = list(map(int, ocgs))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5004: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5005:         p0 = text.find("/P/")  # look for /P policy key
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5006:         if p0 < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5007:             policy = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5008:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5009:             p1 = text.find("ff", p0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5010:             if p1 < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5011:                 p1 = text.find("on", p0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5012:             if p1 < 0:  # some irregular syntax
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5013:                 raise ValueError("bad object at xref")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5014:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5015:                 policy = text[p0 + 3 : p1 + 2]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5016: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5017:         p0 = text.find("/VE[")  # look for /VE visibility expression key
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5018:         if p0 < 0:  # no visibility expression found
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5019:             ve = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5020:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5021:             lp = rp = 0  # find end of /VE by finding last ']'.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5022:             p1 = p0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5023:             while lp < 1 or lp != rp:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5024:                 p1 += 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5025:                 if not p1 < textlen:  # some irregular syntax
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5026:                     raise ValueError("bad object at xref")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5027:                 if text[p1] == "[":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5028:                     lp += 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5029:                 if text[p1] == "]":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5030:                     rp += 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5031:             # p1 now positioned at the last "]"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5032:             ve = text[p0 + 3 : p1 + 1]  # the PDF /VE array
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5033:             ve = (
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5034:                 ve.replace("/And", '"and",')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5035:                 .replace("/Not", '"not",')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5036:                 .replace("/Or", '"or",')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5037:             )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5038:             ve = ve.replace(" 0 R]", "]").replace(" 0 R", ",").replace("][", "],[")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5039:             import json
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5040:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5041:                 ve = json.loads(ve)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5042:             except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5043:                 exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5044:                 message(f"bad /VE key: {ve!r}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5045:                 raise
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5046:         return {"xref": xref, "ocgs": ocgs, "policy": policy, "ve": ve}
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5047:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5049:         """Get list of outline xref numbers."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5050:         xrefs = []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5051:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self, required=0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5052:         if not pdf.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5053:             return xrefs

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5214:     def get_toc(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5215:             doc: 'Document',
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5216:             simple: bool = True,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5217:             ) -> list:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5218:         """Create a table of contents.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5219: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5220:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5221:             simple: a bool to control output. Returns a list, where each entry consists of outline level, title, page number and link destination (if simple = False). For details see PyMuPDF's documentation.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5222:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5223:         def recurse(olItem, liste, lvl):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5224:             """Recursively follow the outline item chain and record item information in a list."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5225:             while olItem and olItem.this.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5226:                 if olItem.title:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5227:                     title = olItem.title
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5228:                 else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5229:                     title = " "
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5230: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5231:                 if not olItem.is_external:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5232:                     if olItem.uri:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5233:                         if olItem.page == -1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5234:                             resolve = doc.resolve_link(olItem.uri)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5235:                             page = resolve[0] + 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5236:                         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5237:                             page = olItem.page + 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5238:                     else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5239:                         page = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5240:                 else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5241:                     page = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5242: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5243:                 if not simple:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5244:                     link = utils.getLinkDict(olItem, doc)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5245:                     liste.append([lvl, title, page, link])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5246:                 else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5247:                     liste.append([lvl, title, page])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5248: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5249:                 if olItem.down:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5250:                     liste = recurse(olItem.down, liste, lvl + 1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5251:                 olItem = olItem.next
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5252:             return liste
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5253: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5254:         # ensure document is open
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5255:         if doc.is_closed:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5256:             raise ValueError("document closed")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5257:         doc.init_doc()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5258:         olItem = doc.outline
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5259:         if not olItem:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5260:             return []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5261:         lvl = 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5262:         liste = []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5263:         toc = recurse(olItem, liste, lvl)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5264:         if doc.is_pdf and not simple:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5265:             doc._extend_toc_items(toc)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5266:         return toc

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5405:     def insert_pdf(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5406:             self,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5407:             docsrc,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5408:             *,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5409:             from_page=-1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5410:             to_page=-1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5411:             start_at=-1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5412:             rotate=-1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5413:             links=1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5414:             annots=1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5415:             widgets=1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5416:             join_duplicates=0,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5417:             show_progress=0,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5418:             final=1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5419:             _gmap=None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5420:             ):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5421:         """Insert a page range from another PDF.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5422: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5423:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5424:             docsrc: PDF to copy from. Must be different object, but may be same file.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5425:             from_page: (int) first source page to copy, 0-based, default 0.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5426:             to_page: (int) last source page to copy, 0-based, default last page.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5427:             start_at: (int) from_page will become this page number in target.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5428:             rotate: (int) rotate copied pages, default -1 is no change.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5429:             links: (int/bool) whether to also copy links.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5430:             annots: (int/bool) whether to also copy annotations.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5431:             widgets: (int/bool) whether to also copy form fields.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5432:             join_duplicates: (int/bool) join or rename duplicate widget names.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5433:             show_progress: (int) progress message interval, 0 is no messages.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5434:             final: (bool) indicates last insertion from this source PDF.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5435:             _gmap: internal use only
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5436:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:5818:     @property

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6053:     def pages(self, start: OptInt =None, stop: OptInt =None, step: OptInt =None) -> collections.abc.Iterable[Page]:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6054:         """Return a generator iterator over a page range.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6055: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6056:         Arguments have the same meaning as for the range() built-in.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6057:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6058:         if not self.page_count:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6059:             return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6060:         # set the start value
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6061:         start = start or 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6062:         while start < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6063:             start += self.page_count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6064:         if start not in range(self.page_count):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6065:             raise ValueError("bad start page number")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6066: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6067:         # set the stop value
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6068:         stop = stop if stop is not None and stop <= self.page_count else self.page_count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6069: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6070:         # set the step value
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6071:         if step == 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6072:             raise ValueError("arg 3 must not be zero")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6073:         if step is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6074:             if start > stop:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6075:                 step = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6076:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6077:                 step = 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6078: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6079:         for pno in range(start, stop, step):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6080:             yield (self.load_page(pno))

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6137:     def reload_page(self, page: Page) -> Page:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6138:         """Make a fresh copy of a page."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6139:         old_annots = {}  # copy annot references to here
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6140:         pno = page.number  # save the page number
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6141:         for k, v in page._annot_refs.items():  # save the annot dictionary
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6142:             old_annots[k] = v
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6143:         
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6144:         # When we call `self.load_page()` below, it will end up in
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6145:         # fz_load_chapter_page(), which will return any matching page in the
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6146:         # document's list of non-ref-counted loaded pages, instead of actually
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6147:         # reloading the page.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6148:         #
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6149:         # We want to assert that we have actually reloaded the fz_page, and not
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6150:         # simply returned the same `fz_page*` pointer from the document's list
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6151:         # of non-ref-counted loaded pages.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6152:         #
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6153:         # So we first remove our reference to the `fz_page*`. This will
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6154:         # decrement .refs, and if .refs was 1, this is guaranteed to free the
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6155:         # `fz_page*` and remove it from the document's list if it was there. So
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6156:         # we are guaranteed that our returned `fz_page*` is from a genuine
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6157:         # reload, even if it happens to reuse the original block of memory.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6158:         #
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6159:         # However if the original .refs is greater than one, there must be
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6160:         # other references to the `fz_page` somewhere, and we require that
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6161:         # these other references are not keeping the page in the document's
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6162:         # list.  We check that we are returning a newly loaded page by
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6163:         # asserting that our returned `fz_page*` is different from the original
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6164:         # `fz_page*` - the original was not freed, so a new `fz_page` cannot
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6165:         # reuse the same block of memory.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6166:         #
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6167:         
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6168:         refs_old = page.this.m_internal.refs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6169:         m_internal_old = page.this.m_internal_value()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6170:         
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6171:         page.this = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6172:         page._erase()  # remove the page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6173:         page = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6174:         TOOLS.store_shrink(100)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6175:         page = self.load_page(pno)  # reload the page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6176: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6177:         # copy annot refs over to the new dictionary
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6178:         #page_proxy = weakref.proxy(page)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6179:         for k, v in old_annots.items():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6180:             annot = old_annots[k]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6181:             #annot.parent = page_proxy  # refresh parent to new page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6182:             page._annot_refs[k] = annot
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6183:         if refs_old == 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6184:             # We know that `page.this = None` will have decremented the ref
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6185:             # count to zero so we are guaranteed that the new `fz_page` is a
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6186:             # new page even if it happens to have reused the same block of
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6187:             # memory.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6188:             pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6189:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6190:             # Check that the new `fz_page*` is different from the original.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6191:             m_internal_new = page.this.m_internal_value()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6192:             assert m_internal_new != m_internal_old, \
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6193:                     f'{refs_old=} {m_internal_old=:#x} {m_internal_new=:#x}'
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6194:         return page
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6195:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6204:     def resolve_link(self, uri=None, chapters=0):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6205:         """Calculate internal link destination.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6206: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6207:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6208:             uri: (str) some Link.uri
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6209:             chapters: (bool) whether to use (chapter, page) format
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6210:         Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6211:             (page_id, x, y) where x, y are point coordinates on the page.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6212:             page_id is either page number (if chapters=0), or (chapter, pno).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6213:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6214:         if not uri:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6215:             if chapters:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6216:                 return (-1, -1), 0, 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6217:             return -1, 0, 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6218:         try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6219:             loc, xp, yp = mupdf.fz_resolve_link(self.this, uri)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6220:         except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6221:             if g_exceptions_verbose:    exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6222:             if chapters:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6223:                 return (-1, -1), 0, 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6224:             return -1, 0, 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6225:         if chapters:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6226:             return (loc.chapter, loc.page), xp, yp
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6227:         pno = mupdf.fz_page_number_from_location(self.this, loc)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6228:         return pno, xp, yp

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6230:     def rewrite_images(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6231:         self,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6232:         dpi_threshold=None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6233:         dpi_target=0,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6234:         quality=0,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6235:         lossy=True,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6236:         lossless=True,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6237:         bitonal=True,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6238:         color=True,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6239:         gray=True,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6240:         set_to_gray=False,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6241:         options=None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6242:     ):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6243:         """Rewrite images in a PDF document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6244: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6245:         The typical use case is to reduce the size of the PDF by recompressing
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6246:         images. Default parameters will convert all images to JPEG where
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6247:         possible, using the specified resolutions and quality. Exclude
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6248:         undesired images by setting parameters to False.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6249:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6250:             dpi_threshold: look at images with a larger DPI only.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6251:             dpi_target: change eligible images to this DPI.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6252:             quality: Quality of the recompressed images (0-100).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6253:             lossy: process lossy image types (e.g. JPEG).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6254:             lossless: process lossless image types (e.g. PNG).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6255:             bitonal: process black-and-white images (e.g. FAX)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6256:             color: process colored images.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6257:             gray: process gray images.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6258:             set_to_gray: whether to change the PDF to gray at process start.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6259:             options: (PdfImageRewriterOptions) Custom options for image
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6260:                     rewriting (optional). Expert use only. If provided, other
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6261:                     parameters are ignored, except set_to_gray.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6262:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6263:         quality_str = str(quality)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6264:         if not dpi_threshold:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6330:     def resolve_names(self):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6331:         """Convert the PDF's destination names into a Python dict.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6332: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6333:         The only parameter is the pymupdf.Document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6334:         All names found in the catalog under keys "/Dests" and "/Names/Dests" are
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6335:         being included.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6336: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6337:         Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6338:             A dcitionary with the following layout:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6339:             - key: (str) the name
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6340:             - value: (dict) with the following layout:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6341:                 * "page":  target page number (0-based). If no page number found -1.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6342:                 * "to": (x, y) target point on page - currently in PDF coordinates,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6343:                         i.e. point (0,0) is the bottom-left of the page.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6344:                 * "zoom": (float) the zoom factor
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6345:                 * "dest": (str) only occurs if the target location on the page has
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6346:                         not been provided as "/XYZ" or if no page number was found.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6347:             Examples:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6348:             {'__bookmark_1': {'page': 0, 'to': (0.0, 541.0), 'zoom': 0.0},
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6349:             '__bookmark_2': {'page': 0, 'to': (0.0, 481.45), 'zoom': 0.0}}
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6350: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6351:             or
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6352: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6353:             '21154a7c20684ceb91f9c9adc3b677c40': {'page': -1, 'dest': '/XYZ 15.75 1486 0'}, ...
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6354:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6355:         if hasattr(self, "_resolved_names"):  # do not execute multiple times!
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6356:             return self._resolved_names

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6358:         page_xrefs = {self.page_xref(i): i for i in range(self.page_count)}
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6359: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6360:         def obj_string(obj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6361:             """Return string version of a PDF object definition."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6362:             buffer = mupdf.fz_new_buffer(512)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6363:             output = mupdf.FzOutput(buffer)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6364:             mupdf.pdf_print_obj(output, obj, 1, 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6365:             output.fz_close_output()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6366:             return JM_UnicodeFromBuffer(buffer)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6368:         def get_array(val):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6369:             """Generate value of one item of the names dictionary."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6370:             templ_dict = {"page": -1, "dest": ""}  # value template
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6371:             if val.pdf_is_indirect():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6372:                 val = mupdf.pdf_resolve_indirect(val)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6373:             if val.pdf_is_array():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6374:                 array = obj_string(val)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6375:             elif val.pdf_is_dict():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6376:                 array = obj_string(mupdf.pdf_dict_gets(val, "D"))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6377:             else:  # if all fails return the empty template
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6378:                 return templ_dict
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6379: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6380:             # replace PDF "null" by zero, omit the square brackets
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6381:             array = array.replace("null", "0")[1:-1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6382: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6383:             # find stuff before first "/"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6384:             idx = array.find("/")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6385:             if idx < 1:  # this has no target page spec
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6386:                 templ_dict["dest"] = array  # return the orig. string
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6387:                 return templ_dict
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6388: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6389:             subval = array[:idx].strip()  # stuff before "/"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6390:             array = array[idx:]  # stuff from "/" onwards
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6391:             templ_dict["dest"] = array
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6392:             # if we start with /XYZ: extract x, y, zoom
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6393:             # 1, 2 or 3 of these values may actually be supplied
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6394:             if array.startswith("/XYZ"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6395:                 del templ_dict["dest"]  # don't return orig string in this case
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6396: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6397:                 # make a list of the 3 tokens following "/XYZ"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6398:                 array_list = array.split()[1:4]  # omit "/XYZ"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6399: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6400:                 # fill up missing tokens with "0" strings
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6401:                 while len(array_list) < 3:  # fill up if too short
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6402:                     array_list.append("0")  # add missing values
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6403: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6404:                 # make list of 3 floats: x, y and zoom
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6405:                 t = list(map(float, array_list))  # the resulting x, y, z values
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6406:                 templ_dict["to"] = (t[0], t[1])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6407:                 templ_dict["zoom"] = t[2]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6408: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6409:             # extract page number
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6410:             if subval.endswith("0 R"):  # page xref given?
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6411:                 templ_dict["page"] = page_xrefs.get(int(subval.split()[0]),-1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6412:             else:  # naked page number given
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6413:                 templ_dict["page"] = int(subval)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6414:             return templ_dict

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6441:         catalog = mupdf.pdf_dict_gets(mupdf.pdf_trailer(pdf), "Root")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6442:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6447:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6577:     # Remove potentially sensitive data from a PDF. Similar to the Adobe

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6580:     def scrub(

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6595:             ) -> None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6596:         
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6597:         def remove_hidden(cont_lines):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6598:             """Remove hidden text from a PDF page.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6599: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6600:             Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6601:                 cont_lines: list of lines with /Contents content. Should have status
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6602:                     from after page.cleanContents().
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6603: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6604:             Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6605:                 List of /Contents lines from which hidden text has been removed.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6606: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6607:             Notes:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6608:                 The input must have been created after the page's /Contents object(s)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6609:                 have been cleaned with page.cleanContents(). This ensures a standard
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6610:                 formatting: one command per line, single spaces between operators.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6611:                 This allows for drastic simplification of this code.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6612:             """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6613:             out_lines = []  # will return this
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6614:             in_text = False  # indicate if within BT/ET object
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6615:             suppress = False  # indicate text suppression active
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6616:             make_return = False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6617:             for line in cont_lines:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6618:                 if line == b"BT":  # start of text object
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6619:                     in_text = True  # switch on
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6620:                     out_lines.append(line)  # output it
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6621:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6622:                 if line == b"ET":  # end of text object
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6623:                     in_text = False  # switch off
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6624:                     out_lines.append(line)  # output it
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6625:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6626:                 if line == b"3 Tr":  # text suppression operator
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6627:                     suppress = True  # switch on
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6628:                     make_return = True
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6629:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6630:                 if line[-2:] == b"Tr" and line[0] != b"3":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6631:                     suppress = False  # text rendering changed
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6632:                     out_lines.append(line)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6633:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6634:                 if line == b"Q":  # unstack command also switches off
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6635:                     suppress = False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6636:                     out_lines.append(line)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6637:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6638:                 if suppress and in_text:  # suppress hidden lines
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6639:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6640:                 out_lines.append(line)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6641:             if make_return:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6642:                 return out_lines
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6643:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6644:                 return None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6645: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6646:         if not doc.is_pdf:  # only works for PDF
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6647:             raise ValueError("is no PDF")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6648:         if doc.is_encrypted or doc.is_closed:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6649:             raise ValueError("closed or encrypted doc")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6650: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6651:         if not clean_pages:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6652:             hidden_text = False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6653:             redactions = False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6654: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6655:         if metadata:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6656:             doc.set_metadata({})  # remove standard metadata
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6657: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6658:         for page in doc:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6659:             if reset_fields:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6660:                 # reset form fields (widgets)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6661:                 for widget in page.widgets():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6662:                     widget.reset()

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6792:         # which, for example, breaks test_3705.

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6889:     def set_markinfo(self, markinfo: dict) -> bool:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6973:     def set_oc(doc: 'Document', xref: int, oc: int) -> None:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6983:         if t != "name" or name not in ("/Image", "/Form"):

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6995:     def set_ocmd(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6996:             doc: 'Document',
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6997:             xref: int = 0,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6998:             ocgs: typing.Union[list, None] = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:6999:             policy: OptStr = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7000:             ve: typing.Union[list, None] = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7001:             ) -> int:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7002:         """Create or update an OCMD object in a PDF document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7003: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7004:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7005:             xref: (int) 0 for creating a new object, otherwise update existing one.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7006:             ocgs: (list) OCG xref numbers, which shall be subject to 'policy'.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7007:             policy: one of 'AllOn', 'AllOff', 'AnyOn', 'AnyOff' (any casing).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7008:             ve: (list) visibility expression. Use instead of 'ocgs' with 'policy'.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7009: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7010:         Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7011:             Xref of the created or updated OCMD.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7012:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7013: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7014:         all_ocgs = set(doc.get_ocgs().keys())
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7015: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7016:         def ve_maker(ve):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7017:             if type(ve) not in (list, tuple) or len(ve) < 2:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7018:                 raise ValueError(f"bad 've' format: {ve}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7019:             if ve[0].lower() not in ("and", "or", "not"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7020:                 raise ValueError(f"bad operand: {ve[0]}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7021:             if ve[0].lower() == "not" and len(ve) != 2:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7022:                 raise ValueError(f"bad 've' format: {ve}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7023:             item = f"[/{ve[0].title()}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7024:             for x in ve[1:]:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7025:                 if type(x) is int:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7026:                     if x not in all_ocgs:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7027:                         raise ValueError(f"bad OCG {x}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7028:                     item += f" {x} 0 R"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7029:                 else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7030:                     item += f" {ve_maker(x)}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7031:             item += "]"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7032:             return item
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7033: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7034:         text = "<</Type/OCMD"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7035: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7036:         if ocgs and type(ocgs) in (list, tuple):  # some OCGs are provided
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7037:             s = set(ocgs).difference(all_ocgs)  # contains illegal xrefs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7038:             if s != set():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7039:                 msg = f"bad OCGs: {s}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7040:                 raise ValueError(msg)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7041:             text += "/OCGs[" + " ".join(map(lambda x: f"{x} 0 R", ocgs)) + "]"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7042: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7043:         if policy:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7044:             policy = str(policy).lower()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7045:             pols = {
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7046:                 "anyon": "AnyOn",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7047:                 "allon": "AllOn",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7048:                 "anyoff": "AnyOff",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7049:                 "alloff": "AllOff",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7050:             }
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7051:             if policy not in ("anyon", "allon", "anyoff", "alloff"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7052:                 raise ValueError(f"bad policy: {policy}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7053:             text += f"/P/{pols[policy]}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7054: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7055:         if ve:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7056:             text += f"/VE{ve_maker(ve)}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7057: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7058:         text += ">>"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7059: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7060:         # make new object or replace old OCMD (check type first)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7061:         if xref == 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7062:             xref = doc.get_new_xref()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7063:         elif "/Type/OCMD" not in doc.xref_object(xref, compressed=True):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7064:             raise ValueError("bad xref or not an OCMD")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7065:         doc.update_object(xref, text)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7066:         return xref

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7068:     def set_pagelayout(self, pagelayout: str):

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7100:     def set_page_labels(doc, labels):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7101:         """Add / replace page label definitions in PDF document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7102: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7103:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7104:             doc: PDF document (resp. 'self').
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7105:             labels: list of label dictionaries like:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7106:             {'startpage': int, 'prefix': str, 'style': str, 'firstpagenum': int},
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7107:             as returned by get_page_labels().
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7108:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7109:         # William Chapman, 2021-01-06
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7110: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7111:         def create_label_str(label):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7112:             """Convert Python label dict to corresponding PDF rule string.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7113: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7114:             Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7115:                 label: (dict) build rule for the label.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7116:             Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7117:                 PDF label rule string wrapped in "<<", ">>".
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7118:             """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7119:             s = f"{label['startpage']}<<"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7120:             if label.get("prefix", "") != "":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7121:                 s += f"/P({label['prefix']})"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7122:             if label.get("style", "") != "":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7123:                 s += f"/S/{label['style']}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7124:             if label.get("firstpagenum", 1) > 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7125:                 s += f"/St {label['firstpagenum']}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7126:             s += ">>"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7127:             return s
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7128: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7129:         def create_nums(labels):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7130:             """Return concatenated string of all labels rules.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7131: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7132:             Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7133:                 labels: (list) dictionaries as created by function 'rule_dict'.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7134:             Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7135:                 PDF compatible string for page label definitions, ready to be
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7136:                 enclosed in PDF array 'Nums[...]'.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7137:             """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7138:             labels.sort(key=lambda x: x["startpage"])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7139:             s = "".join([create_label_str(label) for label in labels])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7140:             return s
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7141: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7142:         doc._set_page_labels(create_nums(labels))
