DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7144:     def set_toc(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7145:             doc: 'Document',
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7146:             toc: list,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7147:             collapse: int = 1,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7148:             ) -> int:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7149:         """Create new outline tree (table of contents, TOC).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7150: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7151:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7152:             toc: (list, tuple) each entry must contain level, title, page and
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7153:                 optionally top margin on the page. None or '()' remove the TOC.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7154:             collapse: (int) collapses entries beyond this level. Zero or None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7155:                 shows all entries unfolded.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7156:         Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7157:             the number of inserted items, or the number of removed items respectively.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7158:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7159:         if doc.is_closed or doc.is_encrypted:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7160:             raise ValueError("document closed or encrypted")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7161:         if not doc.is_pdf:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7162:             raise ValueError("is no PDF")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7163:         if not toc:  # remove all entries
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7164:             return len(doc._delToC())
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7165: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7166:         # validity checks --------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7167:         if type(toc) not in (list, tuple):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7168:             raise ValueError("'toc' must be list or tuple")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7169:         toclen = len(toc)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7170:         page_count = doc.page_count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7171:         t0 = toc[0]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7172:         if type(t0) not in (list, tuple):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7173:             raise ValueError("items must be sequences of 3 or 4 items")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7174:         if t0[0] != 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7175:             raise ValueError("hierarchy level of item 0 must be 1")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7176:         for i in list(range(toclen - 1)):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7177:             t1 = toc[i]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7178:             t2 = toc[i + 1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7179:             if not -1 <= t1[2] <= page_count:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7180:                 raise ValueError(f"row {i}: page number out of range")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7181:             if (type(t2) not in (list, tuple)) or len(t2) not in (3, 4):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7182:                 raise ValueError(f"bad row {(i + 1)}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7183:             if (type(t2[0]) is not int) or t2[0] < 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7184:                 raise ValueError(f"bad hierarchy level in row {(i + 1)}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7185:             if t2[0] > t1[0] + 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7186:                 raise ValueError(f"bad hierarchy level in row {(i + 1)}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7187:         # no formal errors in toc --------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7188: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7189:         # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7190:         # make a list of xref numbers, which we can use for our TOC entries
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7191:         # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7192:         old_xrefs = doc._delToC()  # del old outlines, get their xref numbers
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7193: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7194:         # prepare table of xrefs for new bookmarks
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7195:         old_xrefs = []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7196:         xref = [0] + old_xrefs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7197:         xref[0] = doc._getOLRootNumber()  # entry zero is outline root xref number
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7198:         if toclen > len(old_xrefs):  # too few old xrefs?
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7199:             for i in range((toclen - len(old_xrefs))):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7200:                 xref.append(doc.get_new_xref())  # acquire new ones
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7201: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7202:         lvltab = {0: 0}  # to store last entry per hierarchy level
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7203: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7204:         # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7205:         # contains new outline objects as strings - first one is the outline root
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7206:         # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7207:         olitems = [{"count": 0, "first": -1, "last": -1, "xref": xref[0]}]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7208:         # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7209:         # build olitems as a list of PDF-like connected dictionaries
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7210:         # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7211:         for i in range(toclen):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7212:             o = toc[i]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7213:             lvl = o[0]  # level
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7214:             title = get_pdf_str(o[1])  # title
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7215:             pno = min(doc.page_count - 1, max(0, o[2] - 1))  # page number
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7216:             page_xref = doc.page_xref(pno)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7217:             page_height = doc.page_cropbox(pno).height
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7218:             top = Point(72, page_height - 36)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7219:             dest_dict = {"to": top, "kind": LINK_GOTO}  # fall back target
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7220:             if o[2] < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7221:                 dest_dict["kind"] = LINK_NONE
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7222:             if len(o) > 3:  # some target is specified
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7223:                 if type(o[3]) in (int, float):  # convert a number to a point
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7224:                     dest_dict["to"] = Point(72, page_height - o[3])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7225:                 else:  # if something else, make sure we have a dict
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7226:                     # We make a copy of o[3] to avoid modifying our caller's data.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7227:                     dest_dict = o[3].copy() if type(o[3]) is dict else dest_dict
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7228:                     if "to" not in dest_dict:  # target point not in dict?
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7229:                         dest_dict["to"] = top  # put default in
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7230:                     else:  # transform target to PDF coordinates
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7231:                         page = doc[pno]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7232:                         point = Point(dest_dict["to"])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7233:                         point.y = page.cropbox.height - point.y
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7234:                         point = point * page.rotation_matrix
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7235:                         dest_dict["to"] = (point.x, point.y)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7236:             d = {}
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7237:             d["first"] = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7238:             d["count"] = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7239:             d["last"] = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7240:             d["prev"] = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7241:             d["next"] = -1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7242:             d["dest"] = utils.getDestStr(page_xref, dest_dict)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7243:             d["top"] = dest_dict["to"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7244:             d["title"] = title
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7245:             d["parent"] = lvltab[lvl - 1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7246:             d["xref"] = xref[i + 1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7247:             d["color"] = dest_dict.get("color")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7248:             d["flags"] = dest_dict.get("italic", 0) + 2 * dest_dict.get("bold", 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7249:             lvltab[lvl] = i + 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7250:             parent = olitems[lvltab[lvl - 1]]  # the parent entry
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7251: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7252:             if (
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7253:                 dest_dict.get("collapse") or collapse and lvl > collapse
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7254:             ):  # suppress expansion
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7255:                 parent["count"] -= 1  # make /Count negative
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7256:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7257:                 parent["count"] += 1  # positive /Count
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7258: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7259:             if parent["first"] == -1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7260:                 parent["first"] = i + 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7261:                 parent["last"] = i + 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7262:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7263:                 d["prev"] = parent["last"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7264:                 prev = olitems[parent["last"]]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7265:                 prev["next"] = i + 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7266:                 parent["last"] = i + 1
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7267:             olitems.append(d)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7268: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7269:         # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7270:         # now create each outline item as a string and insert it in the PDF
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7271:         # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7272:         for i, ol in enumerate(olitems):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7273:             txt = "<<"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7274:             if ol["count"] != 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7275:                 txt += f"/Count {ol['count']}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7276:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7277:                 txt += ol["dest"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7278:             except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7279:                 # Verbose in PyMuPDF/tests.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7280:                 if g_exceptions_verbose >= 2:   exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7281:                 pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7282:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7283:                 if ol["first"] > -1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7284:                     txt += f"/First {xref[ol['first']]} 0 R"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7285:             except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7286:                 if g_exceptions_verbose >= 2:   exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7287:                 pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7288:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7289:                 if ol["last"] > -1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7290:                     txt += f"/Last {xref[ol['last']]} 0 R"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7291:             except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7292:                 if g_exceptions_verbose >= 2:   exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7293:                 pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7294:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7295:                 if ol["next"] > -1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7296:                     txt += f"/Next {xref[ol['next']]} 0 R"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7297:             except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7298:                 # Verbose in PyMuPDF/tests.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7299:                 if g_exceptions_verbose >= 2:   exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7300:                 pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7301:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7302:                 if ol["parent"] > -1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7303:                     txt += f"/Parent {xref[ol['parent']]} 0 R"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7304:             except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7305:                 # Verbose in PyMuPDF/tests.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7306:                 if g_exceptions_verbose >= 2:   exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7307:                 pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7308:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7309:                 if ol["prev"] > -1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7310:                     txt += f"/Prev {xref[ol['prev']]} 0 R"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7311:             except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7312:                 # Verbose in PyMuPDF/tests.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7313:                 if g_exceptions_verbose >= 2:   exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7314:                 pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7315:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7316:                 txt += "/Title" + ol["title"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7317:             except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7318:                 # Verbose in PyMuPDF/tests.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7319:                 if g_exceptions_verbose >= 2:   exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7320:                 pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7321: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7322:             if ol.get("color") and len(ol["color"]) == 3:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7323:                 txt += f"/C[ {_format_g(tuple(ol['color']))}]"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7324:             if ol.get("flags", 0) > 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7325:                 txt += f"/F {ol['flags']}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7326:

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7328:                 txt += "/Type/Outlines"  # so add the /Type entry

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7330:             doc.update_object(xref[i], txt)  # insert the PDF object

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7335:     def set_toc_item(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7336:             doc: 'Document',
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7337:             idx: int,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7338:             dest_dict: OptDict = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7339:             kind: OptInt = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7340:             pno: OptInt = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7341:             uri: OptStr = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7342:             title: OptStr = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7343:             to: point_like = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7344:             filename: OptStr = None,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7345:             zoom: float = 0,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7346:             ) -> None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7347:         """Update TOC item by index.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7348: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7349:         It allows changing the item's title and link destination.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7350: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7351:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7352:             idx:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7353:                 (int) desired index of the TOC list, as created by get_toc.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7354:             dest_dict:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7355:                 (dict) destination dictionary as created by get_toc(False).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7356:                 Outrules all other parameters. If None, the remaining parameters
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7357:                 are used to make a dest dictionary.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7358:             kind:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7359:                 (int) kind of link (pymupdf.LINK_GOTO, etc.). If None, then only
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7360:                 the title will be updated. If pymupdf.LINK_NONE, the TOC item will
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7361:                 be deleted.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7362:             pno:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7363:                 (int) page number (1-based like in get_toc). Required if
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7364:                 pymupdf.LINK_GOTO.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7365:             uri:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7366:                 (str) the URL, required if pymupdf.LINK_URI.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7367:             title:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7368:                 (str) the new title. No change if None.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7369:             to:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7370:                 (point-like) destination on the target page. If omitted, (72, 36)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7371:                 will be used as target coordinates.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7372:             filename:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7373:                 (str) destination filename, required for pymupdf.LINK_GOTOR and
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7374:                 pymupdf.LINK_LAUNCH.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7375:             name:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7376:                 (str) a destination name for pymupdf.LINK_NAMED.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7377:             zoom:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7378:                 (float) a zoom factor for the target location (pymupdf.LINK_GOTO).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7379:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7380:         xref = doc.get_outline_xrefs()[idx]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7381:         page_xref = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7382:         if type(dest_dict) is dict:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7383:             if dest_dict["kind"] == LINK_GOTO:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7384:                 pno = dest_dict["page"]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7385:                 page_xref = doc.page_xref(pno)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7386:                 page_height = doc.page_cropbox(pno).height
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7387:                 to = dest_dict.get('to', Point(72, 36))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7388:                 to.y = page_height - to.y
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7389:                 dest_dict["to"] = to
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7390:             action = utils.getDestStr(page_xref, dest_dict)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7391:             if not action.startswith("/A"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7392:                 raise ValueError("bad bookmark dest")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7393:             color = dest_dict.get("color")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7394:             if color:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7395:                 color = list(map(float, color))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7396:                 if len(color) != 3 or min(color) < 0 or max(color) > 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7397:                     raise ValueError("bad color value")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7398:             bold = dest_dict.get("bold", False)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7399:             italic = dest_dict.get("italic", False)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7400:             flags = italic + 2 * bold
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7401:             collapse = dest_dict.get("collapse")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7402:             return doc._update_toc_item(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7403:                 xref,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7404:                 action=action[2:],
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7405:                 title=title,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7406:                 color=color,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7407:                 flags=flags,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7408:                 collapse=collapse,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7409:             )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7410: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7411:         if kind == LINK_NONE:  # delete bookmark item
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7412:             return doc.del_toc_item(idx)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7413:         if kind is None and title is None:  # treat as no-op
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7414:             return None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7415:         if kind is None:  # only update title text
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7416:             return doc._update_toc_item(xref, action=None, title=title)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7417: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7418:         if kind == LINK_GOTO:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7419:             if pno is None or pno not in range(1, doc.page_count + 1):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7420:                 raise ValueError("bad page number")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7421:             page_xref = doc.page_xref(pno - 1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7422:             page_height = doc.page_cropbox(pno - 1).height
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7423:             if to is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7424:                 to = Point(72, page_height - 36)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7425:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7426:                 to = Point(to)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7427:                 to.y = page_height - to.y
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7428: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7429:         ddict = {
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7430:             "kind": kind,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7431:             "to": to,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7432:             "uri": uri,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7433:             "page": pno,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7434:             "file": filename,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7435:             "zoom": zoom,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7436:         }
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7437:         action = utils.getDestStr(page_xref, ddict)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7438:         if action == "" or not action.startswith("/A"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7439:             raise ValueError("bad bookmark dest")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7440: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7441:         return doc._update_toc_item(xref, action=action[2:], title=title)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7442: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7443:     def set_xml_metadata(self, metadata):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7444:         """Store XML document level metadata."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7445:         if self.is_closed or self.is_encrypted:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7446:             raise ValueError("document closed or encrypted")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7447:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7448:         root = mupdf.pdf_dict_get( mupdf.pdf_trailer( pdf), PDF_NAME('Root'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7449:         if not root.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7450:             RAISEPY( MSG_BAD_PDFROOT, JM_Exc_FileDataError)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7451:         res = mupdf.fz_new_buffer_from_copied_data( metadata.encode('utf-8'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7452:         xml = mupdf.pdf_dict_get( root, PDF_NAME('Metadata'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7453:         if xml.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7454:             JM_update_stream( pdf, xml, res, 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7455:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7456:             xml = mupdf.pdf_add_stream( pdf, res, mupdf.PdfObj(), 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7457:             mupdf.pdf_dict_put( xml, PDF_NAME('Type'), PDF_NAME('Metadata'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7458:             mupdf.pdf_dict_put( xml, PDF_NAME('Subtype'), PDF_NAME('XML'))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7459:             mupdf.pdf_dict_put( root, PDF_NAME('Metadata'), xml)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7461:     def subset_fonts(doc: 'Document', verbose: bool = False, fallback: bool = False) -> OptInt:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7462:         """Build font subsets in a PDF.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7463: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7464:         Eligible fonts are potentially replaced by smaller versions. Page text is
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7465:         NOT rewritten and thus should retain properties like being hidden or
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7466:         controlled by optional content.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7467: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7468:         This method by default uses MuPDF's own internal feature to create subset
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7469:         fonts. As this is a new function, errors may still occur. In this case,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7470:         please fall back to using the previous version by using "fallback=True".
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7471:         Fallback mode requires the external package 'fontTools'.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7472: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7473:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7474:             fallback: use the older deprecated implementation.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7475:             verbose: only used by fallback mode.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7476: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7477:         Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7478:             The new MuPDF-based code returns None.  The deprecated fallback
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7479:             mode returns 0 if there are no fonts to subset.  Otherwise, it
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7480:             returns the decrease in fontsize (the difference in fontsize),
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7481:             measured in bytes.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7482:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7483:         # Font binaries: -  "buffer" -> (names, xrefs, (unicodes, glyphs))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7484:         # An embedded font is uniquely defined by its fontbuffer only. It may have
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7485:         # multiple names and xrefs.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7486:         # Once the sets of used unicodes and glyphs are known, we compute a
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7487:         # smaller version of the buffer user package fontTools.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7488: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7489:         if not fallback:  # by default use MuPDF function
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7490:             pdf = mupdf.pdf_document_from_fz_document(doc)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7491:             mupdf.pdf_subset_fonts2(pdf, list(range(doc.page_count)))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7492:             return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7493: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7494:         font_buffers = {}
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7495: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7496:         def get_old_widths(xref):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7497:             """Retrieve old font '/W' and '/DW' values."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7498:             df = doc.xref_get_key(xref, "DescendantFonts")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7499:             if df[0] != "array":  # only handle xref specifications
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7500:                 return None, None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7501:             df_xref = int(df[1][1:-1].replace("0 R", ""))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7502:             widths = doc.xref_get_key(df_xref, "W")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7503:             if widths[0] != "array":  # no widths key found
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7504:                 widths = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7505:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7506:                 widths = widths[1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7507:             dwidths = doc.xref_get_key(df_xref, "DW")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7508:             if dwidths[0] != "int":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7509:                 dwidths = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7510:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7511:                 dwidths = dwidths[1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7512:             return widths, dwidths
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7513: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7514:         def set_old_widths(xref, widths, dwidths):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7515:             """Restore the old '/W' and '/DW' in subsetted font.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7516: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7517:             If either parameter is None or evaluates to False, the corresponding
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7518:             dictionary key will be set to null.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7519:             """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7520:             df = doc.xref_get_key(xref, "DescendantFonts")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7521:             if df[0] != "array":  # only handle xref specs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7522:                 return None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7523:             df_xref = int(df[1][1:-1].replace("0 R", ""))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7524:             if (type(widths) is not str or not widths) and doc.xref_get_key(df_xref, "W")[
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7525:                 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7526:             ] != "null":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7527:                 doc.xref_set_key(df_xref, "W", "null")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7528:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7529:                 doc.xref_set_key(df_xref, "W", widths)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7530:             if (type(dwidths) is not str or not dwidths) and doc.xref_get_key(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7531:                 df_xref, "DW"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7532:             )[0] != "null":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7533:                 doc.xref_set_key(df_xref, "DW", "null")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7534:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7535:                 doc.xref_set_key(df_xref, "DW", dwidths)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7536:             return None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7537: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7538:         def set_subset_fontname(new_xref):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7539:             """Generate a name prefix to tag a font as subset.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7540: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7541:             We use a random generator to select 6 upper case ASCII characters.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7542:             The prefixed name must be put in the font xref as the "/BaseFont" value
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7543:             and in the FontDescriptor object as the '/FontName' value.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7544:             """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7545:             # The following generates a prefix like 'ABCDEF+'
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7546:             import random
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7547:             import string
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7548:             prefix = "".join(random.choices(tuple(string.ascii_uppercase), k=6)) + "+"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7549:             font_str = doc.xref_object(new_xref, compressed=True)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7550:             font_str = font_str.replace("/BaseFont/", "/BaseFont/" + prefix)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7551:             df = doc.xref_get_key(new_xref, "DescendantFonts")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7552:             if df[0] == "array":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7553:                 df_xref = int(df[1][1:-1].replace("0 R", ""))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7554:                 fd = doc.xref_get_key(df_xref, "FontDescriptor")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7555:                 if fd[0] == "xref":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7556:                     fd_xref = int(fd[1].replace("0 R", ""))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7557:                     fd_str = doc.xref_object(fd_xref, compressed=True)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7558:                     fd_str = fd_str.replace("/FontName/", "/FontName/" + prefix)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7559:                     doc.update_object(fd_xref, fd_str)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7560:             doc.update_object(new_xref, font_str)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7561: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7562:         def build_subset(buffer, unc_set, gid_set):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7563:             """Build font subset using fontTools.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7564: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7565:             Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7566:                 buffer: (bytes) the font given as a binary buffer.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7567:                 unc_set: (set) required glyph ids.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7568:             Returns:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7569:                 Either None if subsetting is unsuccessful or the subset font buffer.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7570:             """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7571:             try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7572:                 import fontTools.subset as fts
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7573:             except ImportError:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7574:                 if g_exceptions_verbose:    exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7575:                 message("This method requires fontTools to be installed.")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7576:                 raise
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7577:             import tempfile
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7578:             with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_dir:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7579:                 oldfont_path = f"{tmp_dir}/oldfont.ttf"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7580:                 newfont_path = f"{tmp_dir}/newfont.ttf"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7581:                 uncfile_path = f"{tmp_dir}/uncfile.txt"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7582:                 args = [
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7583:                     oldfont_path,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7584:                     "--retain-gids",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7585:                     f"--output-file={newfont_path}",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7586:                     "--layout-features=*",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7587:                     "--passthrough-tables",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7588:                     "--ignore-missing-glyphs",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7589:                     "--ignore-missing-unicodes",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7590:                     "--symbol-cmap",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7591:                 ]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7592: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7593:                 # store glyph ids or unicodes as file
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7594:                 with io.open(f"{tmp_dir}/uncfile.txt", "w", encoding='utf8') as unc_file:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7595:                     if 0xFFFD in unc_set:  # error unicode exists -> use glyphs
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7596:                         args.append(f"--gids-file={uncfile_path}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7597:                         gid_set.add(189)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7598:                         unc_list = list(gid_set)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7599:                         for unc in unc_list:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7600:                             unc_file.write(f"{unc}\n")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7601:                     else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7602:                         args.append(f"--unicodes-file={uncfile_path}")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7603:                         unc_set.add(255)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7604:                         unc_list = list(unc_set)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7605:                         for unc in unc_list:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7606:                             unc_file.write(f"{unc:04x}\n")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7607: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7608:                 # store fontbuffer as a file
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7609:                 with io.open(oldfont_path, "wb") as fontfile:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7610:                     fontfile.write(buffer)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7611:                 try:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7612:                     os.remove(newfont_path)  # remove old file
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7613:                 except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7614:                     pass
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7615:                 try:  # invoke fontTools subsetter
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7616:                     fts.main(args)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7617:                     font = Font(fontfile=newfont_path)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7618:                     new_buffer = font.buffer  # subset font binary
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7619:                     if font.glyph_count == 0:  # intercept empty font
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7620:                         new_buffer = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7621:                 except Exception:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7622:                     exception_info()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7623:                     new_buffer = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7624:             return new_buffer

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7626:         def repl_fontnames(doc):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7627:             """Populate 'font_buffers'.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7628: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7629:             For each font candidate, store its xref and the list of names
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7630:             by which PDF text may refer to it (there may be multiple).
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7631:             """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7632: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7633:             def norm_name(name):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7634:                 """Recreate font name that contains PDF hex codes.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7635: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7636:                 E.g. #20 -> space, chr(32)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7637:                 """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7638:                 while "#" in name:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7639:                     p = name.find("#")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7640:                     c = int(name[p + 1 : p + 3], 16)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7641:                     name = name.replace(name[p : p + 3], chr(c))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7642:                 return name
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7643: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7644:             def get_fontnames(doc, item):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7645:                 """Return a list of fontnames for an item of page.get_fonts().
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7646: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7647:                 There may be multiple names e.g. for Type0 fonts.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7648:                 """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7649:                 fontname = item[3]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7650:                 names = [fontname]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7651:                 fontname = doc.xref_get_key(item[0], "BaseFont")[1][1:]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7652:                 fontname = norm_name(fontname)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7653:                 if fontname not in names:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7654:                     names.append(fontname)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7655:                 descendents = doc.xref_get_key(item[0], "DescendantFonts")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7656:                 if descendents[0] != "array":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7657:                     return names
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7658:                 descendents = descendents[1][1:-1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7659:                 if descendents.endswith(" 0 R"):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7660:                     xref = int(descendents[:-4])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7661:                     descendents = doc.xref_object(xref, compressed=True)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7662:                 p1 = descendents.find("/BaseFont")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7663:                 if p1 >= 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7664:                     p2 = descendents.find("/", p1 + 1)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7665:                     p1 = min(descendents.find("/", p2 + 1), descendents.find(">>", p2 + 1))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7666:                     fontname = descendents[p2 + 1 : p1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7667:                     fontname = norm_name(fontname)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7668:                     if fontname not in names:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7669:                         names.append(fontname)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7670:                 return names
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7671: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7672:             for i in range(doc.page_count):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7673:                 for f in doc.get_page_fonts(i, full=True):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7674:                     font_xref = f[0]  # font xref
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7675:                     font_ext = f[1]  # font file extension
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7676:                     basename = f[3]  # font basename
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7677: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7678:                     if font_ext not in (  # skip if not supported by fontTools
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7679:                         "otf",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7680:                         "ttf",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7681:                         "woff",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7682:                         "woff2",
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7683:                     ):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7684:                         continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7685:                     # skip fonts which already are subsets
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7686:                     if len(basename) > 6 and basename[6] == "+":
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7687:                         continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7688: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7689:                     extr = doc.extract_font(font_xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7690:                     fontbuffer = extr[-1]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7691:                     names = get_fontnames(doc, f)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7692:                     name_set, xref_set, subsets = font_buffers.get(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7693:                         fontbuffer, (set(), set(), (set(), set()))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7694:                     )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7695:                     xref_set.add(font_xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7696:                     for name in names:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7697:                         name_set.add(name)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7698:                     font = Font(fontbuffer=fontbuffer)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7699:                     name_set.add(font.name)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7700:                     del font
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7701:                     font_buffers[fontbuffer] = (name_set, xref_set, subsets)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7702: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7703:         def find_buffer_by_name(name):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7704:             for buffer, (name_set, _, _) in font_buffers.items():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7705:                 if name in name_set:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7706:                     return buffer
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7707:             return None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7708: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7709:         # -----------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7710:         # main function
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7711:         # -----------------
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7712:         repl_fontnames(doc)  # populate font information
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7713:         if not font_buffers:  # nothing found to do
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7714:             if verbose:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7715:                 message(f'No fonts to subset.')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7716:             return 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7717: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7718:         old_fontsize = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7719:         new_fontsize = 0
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7720:         for fontbuffer in font_buffers.keys():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7721:             old_fontsize += len(fontbuffer)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7722: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7723:         # Scan page text for usage of subsettable fonts
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7724:         for page in doc:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7725:             # go through the text and extend set of used glyphs by font
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7726:             # we use a modified MuPDF trace device, which delivers us glyph ids.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7727:             for span in page.get_texttrace():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7728:                 if type(span) is not dict:  # skip useless information
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7729:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7730:                 fontname = span["font"][:33]  # fontname for the span
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7731:                 buffer = find_buffer_by_name(fontname)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7732:                 if buffer is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7733:                     continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7734:                 name_set, xref_set, (set_ucs, set_gid) = font_buffers[buffer]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7735:                 for c in span["chars"]:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7736:                     set_ucs.add(c[0])  # unicode
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7737:                     set_gid.add(c[1])  # glyph id
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7738:                 font_buffers[buffer] = (name_set, xref_set, (set_ucs, set_gid))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7739: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7740:         # build the font subsets
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7741:         for old_buffer, (name_set, xref_set, subsets) in font_buffers.items():
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7742:             new_buffer = build_subset(old_buffer, subsets[0], subsets[1])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7743:             fontname = list(name_set)[0]
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7744:             if new_buffer is None or len(new_buffer) >= len(old_buffer):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7745:                 # subset was not created or did not get smaller
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7746:                 if verbose:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7747:                     message(f'Cannot subset {fontname!r}.')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7748:                 continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7749:             if verbose:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7750:                 message(f"Built subset of font {fontname!r}.")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7751:             val = doc._insert_font(fontbuffer=new_buffer)  # store subset font in PDF
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7752:             new_xref = val[0]  # get its xref
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7753:             set_subset_fontname(new_xref)  # tag fontname as subset font
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7754:             font_str = doc.xref_object(  # get its object definition
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7755:                 new_xref,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7756:                 compressed=True,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7757:             )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7758:             # walk through the original font xrefs and replace each by the subset def
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7759:             for font_xref in xref_set:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7760:                 # we need the original '/W' and '/DW' width values
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7761:                 width_table, def_width = get_old_widths(font_xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7762:                 # ... and replace original font definition at xref with it
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7763:                 doc.update_object(font_xref, font_str)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7764:                 # now copy over old '/W' and '/DW' values
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7765:                 if width_table or def_width:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7766:                     set_old_widths(font_xref, width_table, def_width)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7767:             # 'new_xref' remains unused in the PDF and must be removed
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7768:             # by garbage collection.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7769:             new_fontsize += len(new_buffer)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7770: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7771:         return old_fontsize - new_fontsize

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7773:     def switch_layer(self, config, as_default=0):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7774:         """Activate an OC layer."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7775:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7776:         cfgs = mupdf.pdf_dict_getl(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7777:                 mupdf.pdf_trailer( pdf),
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7778:                 PDF_NAME('Root'),
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7779:                 PDF_NAME('OCProperties'),
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7780:                 PDF_NAME('Configs')
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7781:                 )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7782:         if not mupdf.pdf_is_array( cfgs) or not mupdf.pdf_array_len( cfgs):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7783:             if config < 1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7784:                 return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7785:             raise ValueError( MSG_BAD_OC_LAYER)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7786:         if config < 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7787:             return
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7788:         mupdf.pdf_select_layer_config( pdf, config)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7789:         if as_default:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7790:             mupdf.pdf_set_layer_config_as_default( pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7791:             mupdf.ll_pdf_read_ocg( pdf.m_internal)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7793:     def update_object(self, xref, text, page=None):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7794:         """Replace object definition source."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7795:         if self.is_closed or self.is_encrypted:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7796:             raise ValueError("document closed or encrypted")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7797:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7798:         xreflen = mupdf.pdf_xref_len(pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7799:         if not _INRANGE(xref, 1, xreflen-1):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7800:             RAISEPY("bad xref", MSG_BAD_XREF)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7801:         ENSURE_OPERATION(pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7802:         # create new object with passed-in string
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7803:         new_obj = JM_pdf_obj_from_str(pdf, text)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7804:         mupdf.pdf_update_object(pdf, xref, new_obj)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7805:         if page:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7806:             JM_refresh_links( _as_pdf_page(page))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7807: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7808:     def update_stream(self, xref=0, stream=None, new=1, compress=1):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7809:         """Replace xref stream part."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7810:         if self.is_closed or self.is_encrypted:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7811:             raise ValueError("document closed or encrypted")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7812:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7813:         xreflen = mupdf.pdf_xref_len(pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7814:         if xref < 1 or xref > xreflen:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7815:             raise ValueError( MSG_BAD_XREF)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7816:         # get the object
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7817:         obj = mupdf.pdf_new_indirect(pdf, xref, 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7818:         if not mupdf.pdf_is_dict(obj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7819:             raise ValueError( MSG_IS_NO_DICT)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7820:         res = JM_BufferFromBytes(stream)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7821:         if not res.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7822:             raise TypeError( MSG_BAD_BUFFER)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7823:         JM_update_stream(pdf, obj, res, compress)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7824:         pdf.dirty = 1

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7826:     @property

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7829:         Count versions of PDF document.

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7832:         if pdf.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7833:             return mupdf.pdf_count_versions(pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7834:         return 0

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7895:     def xref_copy(doc: 'Document', source: int, target: int, *, keep: list = None) -> None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7896:         """Copy a PDF dictionary object to another one given their xref numbers.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7897: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7898:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7899:             doc: PDF document object
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7900:             source: source xref number
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7901:             target: target xref number, the xref must already exist
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7902:             keep: an optional list of 1st level keys in target that should not be
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7903:                   removed before copying.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7904:         Notes:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7905:             This works similar to the copy() method of dictionaries in Python. The
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7906:             source may be a stream object.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7907:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7908:         if doc.xref_is_stream(source):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7909:             # read new xref stream, maintaining compression
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7910:             stream = doc.xref_stream_raw(source)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7911:             doc.update_stream(
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7912:                 target,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7913:                 stream,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7914:                 compress=False,  # keeps source compression
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7915:                 new=True,  # in case target is no stream
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7916:             )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7917: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7918:         # empty the target completely, observe exceptions
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7919:         if keep is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7920:             keep = []
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7921:         for key in doc.xref_get_keys(target):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7922:             if key in keep:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7923:                 continue
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7924:             doc.xref_set_key(target, key, "null")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7925:         # copy over all source dict items
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7926:         for key in doc.xref_get_keys(source):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7927:             item = doc.xref_get_key(source, key)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7928:             doc.xref_set_key(target, key, item[1])
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7929:     
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7930:     def xref_get_key(self, xref, key):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7931:         """Get PDF dict key value of object at 'xref'."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7932:         pdf = _as_pdf_document(self)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7933:         xreflen = mupdf.pdf_xref_len(pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7934:         if not _INRANGE(xref, 1, xreflen-1) and xref != -1:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7935:             raise ValueError( MSG_BAD_XREF)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7936:         if xref > 0:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7937:             obj = mupdf.pdf_load_object(pdf, xref)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7938:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7939:             obj = mupdf.pdf_trailer(pdf)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7940:         if not obj.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7941:             return ("null", "null")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7942:         subobj = mupdf.pdf_dict_getp(obj, key)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7943:         if not subobj.m_internal:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7944:             return ("null", "null")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7945:         text = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7946:         if mupdf.pdf_is_indirect(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7947:             type = "xref"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7948:             text = f"{mupdf.pdf_to_num(subobj):d} 0 R"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7949:         elif mupdf.pdf_is_array(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7950:             type = "array"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7951:         elif mupdf.pdf_is_dict(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7952:             type = "dict"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7953:         elif mupdf.pdf_is_int(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7954:             type = "int"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7955:             text = f"{mupdf.pdf_to_int(subobj):d}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7956:         elif mupdf.pdf_is_real(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7957:             type = "float"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7958:         elif mupdf.pdf_is_null(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7959:             type = "null"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7960:             text = "null"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7961:         elif mupdf.pdf_is_bool(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7962:             type = "bool"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7963:             if mupdf.pdf_to_bool(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7964:                 text = "true"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7965:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7966:                 text = "false"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7967:         elif mupdf.pdf_is_name(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7968:             type = "name"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7969:             text = f"/{mupdf.pdf_to_name(subobj)}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7970:         elif mupdf.pdf_is_string(subobj):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7971:             type = "string"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7972:             text = JM_UnicodeFromStr(mupdf.pdf_to_text_string(subobj))
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7973:         else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7974:             type = "unknown"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7975:         if text is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7976:             res = JM_object_to_buffer(subobj, 1, 0)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7977:             text = JM_UnicodeFromBuffer(res)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7978:         return (type, text)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7980:     def xref_get_keys(self, xref):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7981:         """Get the keys of PDF dict object at 'xref'. Use -1 for the PDF trailer."""

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:7987:             obj = mupdf.pdf_load_object( pdf, xref)

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1195:         """Filters documents by their category in the mime-type attribute.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1196: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1197:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1198:             category (:obj:`str`): Category of the media you want to filter.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1199: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1200:         Example:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1201:             ``filters.Document.Category('audio/')`` returns :obj:`True` for all types
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1202:             of audio sent as a file, for example ``'audio/mpeg'`` or ``'audio/x-wav'``.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1203: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1204:         Note:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1205:             This Filter only filters by the mime_type of the document, it doesn't check the
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1206:             validity of the document. The user can manipulate the mime-type of a message and
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1207:             send media with wrong types that don't fit to this handler.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1208:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1209: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1210:         __slots__ = ("_category",)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1211: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1212:         def __init__(self, category: str):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1213:             self._category = category
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1214:             super().__init__(name=f"filters.Document.Category('{self._category}')")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1215: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1216:         def filter(self, message: Message) -> bool:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1217:             if message.document and message.document.mime_type:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1218:                 return message.document.mime_type.startswith(self._category)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1219:             return False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1220: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1221:     APPLICATION = Category("application/")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1222:     """Use as ``filters.Document.APPLICATION``."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1223:     AUDIO = Category("audio/")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1224:     """Use as ``filters.Document.AUDIO``."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1225:     IMAGE = Category("image/")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1226:     """Use as ``filters.Document.IMAGE``."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1227:     VIDEO = Category("video/")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1228:     """Use as ``filters.Document.VIDEO``."""
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1229:     TEXT = Category("text/")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1230:     """Use as ``filters.Document.TEXT``."""

DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1232:     class FileExtension(MessageFilter):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1233:         """This filter filters documents by their file ending/extension.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1234: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1235:         Args:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1236:             file_extension (:obj:`str` | :obj:`None`): Media file extension you want to filter.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1237:             case_sensitive (:obj:`bool`, optional): Pass :obj:`True` to make the filter case
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1238:                 sensitive. Default: :obj:`False`.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1239: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1240:         Example:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1241:             * ``filters.Document.FileExtension("jpg")``
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1242:               filters files with extension ``".jpg"``.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1243:             * ``filters.Document.FileExtension(".jpg")``
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1244:               filters files with extension ``"..jpg"``.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1245:             * ``filters.Document.FileExtension("Dockerfile", case_sensitive=True)``
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1246:               filters files with extension ``".Dockerfile"`` minding the case.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1247:             * ``filters.Document.FileExtension(None)``
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1248:               filters files without a dot in the filename.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1249: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1250:         Note:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1251:             * This Filter only filters by the file ending/extension of the document,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1252:               it doesn't check the validity of document.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1253:             * The user can manipulate the file extension of a document and
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1254:               send media with wrong types that don't fit to this handler.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1255:             * Case insensitive by default,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1256:               you may change this with the flag ``case_sensitive=True``.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1257:             * Extension should be passed without leading dot
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1258:               unless it's a part of the extension.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1259:             * Pass :obj:`None` to filter files with no extension,
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1260:               i.e. without a dot in the filename.
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1261:         """
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1262: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1263:         __slots__ = ("_file_extension", "is_case_sensitive")
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1264: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1265:         def __init__(self, file_extension: str | None, case_sensitive: bool = False):
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1266:             super().__init__()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1267:             self.is_case_sensitive: bool = case_sensitive
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1268:             if file_extension is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1269:                 self._file_extension = None
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1270:                 self.name = "filters.Document.FileExtension(None)"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1271:             elif self.is_case_sensitive:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1272:                 self._file_extension = f".{file_extension}"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1273:                 self.name = (
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1274:                     f"filters.Document.FileExtension({file_extension!r}, case_sensitive=True)"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1275:                 )
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1276:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1277:                 self._file_extension = f".{file_extension}".lower()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1278:                 self.name = f"filters.Document.FileExtension({file_extension.lower()!r})"
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1279: 
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1280:         def filter(self, message: Message) -> bool:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1281:             if message.document is None or message.document.file_name is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1282:                 return False
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1283:             if self._file_extension is None:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1284:                 return "." not in message.document.file_name
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1285:             if self.is_case_sensitive:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1286:                 filename = message.document.file_name
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1287:             else:
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1288:                 filename = message.document.file_name.lower()
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1289:             return filename.endswith(self._file_extension)
DOCUMENT_SOURCE:1290:
